| 2026-11-06 00:00 UTC | 2026-11-06 02:00 UTC | Football - Spanish league: Rayo Vallecano - Celta 31.10. | Primera or popularly La Liga is the first Spanish football league. With as many as 22 European Championship titles won, the Spanish Premier League has been considered the best European football league for years. Founded in 1929, Primera has 20 teams that try to collect the most points in the 38 rounds of the season. Real Madrid managed to do that the most times, while the second most successful team is Barcelona.
